--\n\nStores tutor available teaching time.\n\nFields:\n\n* id\n* profile_id or application_id depending on current structure\n* day_of_week\n* start_time\n* end_time\n* teaching_mode\n\nTeaching mode:\n\n* online\n* offline\n* both\n\nIf my current schema already has similar tables, do not duplicate unnecessarily. <button\n171\t              type=\"button\"\n172\t              onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}\n173\t              className=\"px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-primary border border-primary rounded-lg hover:bg-primary/5 transition-colors\"\n174\t            >\n175\t              Thay Đổi\n176\t            </button>\n177\t            <button\n178\t              type=\"button\"\n179\t              onClick={onRemove}\n180\t              className=\"px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-error border border-error rounded-lg hover:bg-error/5 transition-colors\"\n181\t            >\n182\t              Xóa\n183\t            </button>\n184\t          </div>\n185\t        </div>\n186\t      ) : (\n187\t        /* ── Empty State ── */\n188\t        <button\n189\t          type=\"button\"\n190\t          onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}\n191\t          className=\"w-full border-2 border-dashed border-outline-variant rounded-lg p-6 className=\"font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant\">JPG, JPEG, PNG, WEBP · Tối đa 5 MB</span>\n196\t        </button>\n197\t      )}\n198\t\n199\t      {error && (\n200\t        <p        </p>\n204\t      )}\n205\t\n206\t      {/* Hidden file input — image only, no PDF */}\n207\t      <input\n208\t        ref={inputRef}\n209\t        id={id}\n210\t        type=\"file\"\n211\t        accept=\"image/jpeg,image/jpg,image/png,image/webp\"\n212\t        className=\"hidden\"\n213\t        onChange={onFileChange}\n214\t      />\n215\t    </div>\n216\t  )\n217\t}\n218\t\n219\t// ─── Field helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n220\t\n221\tfunction FieldError({ msg }) {\n222\t  if (!msg) return null\n223\t  return (\n224\t    <p className=\"mt-1 font-label-sm text-label-sm text-error flex items-center gap-1\">\n225\t      <span className=\"material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]\">error</span>\n226\t      {msg}\n227\t    </p>\n228\t  )\n229\t}\n230\t\n231\tfunction inputCls(hasError) {\n232\t  return `h-12 px-4 rounded-lg border ${\n233\t    hasError ? 'border-error' : 'border-outline'\n234\t  } focus:border-primary focus:ring-0 outline-none transition-all bg-white font-body-md w-full focus:shadow-[0_0_0_3px_rgba(30,64,175,0.15)]`\n235\t}\n236\t\n237\tfunction selectCls(hasError) {\n238\t  return `h-12 px-4 rounded-lg border ${\n239\t    hasError ? 'border-error' : 'border-outline'\n240\t  } focus:border-primary focus:ring-0 outline-none transition-all bg-white font-body-md w-full appearance-none focus:shadow-[0_0_0_3px_rgba(30,64,175,0.15)]`\n241\t}\n242\t\n243\t// ─── Main Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n244\t\n245\texport default function TutorProfileForm() {\n246\t  const { token, loginSilent } = useAuth()\n247\t\n248\t  // Detect new-registration mode (tutor chưa có tài khoản)\n249\t  const pendingRegRaw = sessionStorage.getItem('pendingTutorReg')\n250\t  const pendingReg = pendingRegRaw ? useCallback((e, setFileFn, errorKey) => {\n304\t    const file = e.target.files[0]\n305\t    e.target.value = '' // reset so re-selecting same file works\n306\t    if (!file) return\n307\t    const }))\n313\t    setFileFn(file)\n314\t  }, [])\n315\t\n316\t  // ── Validation per step ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n317\t  const validateStep1 = () => {\n318\t    const e = {}\n319\t    if (!firstName.trim()) e.firstName = 'Tên là bắt buộc.'\n320\t    if (!lastName.trim()) e.lastName = 'Họ là bắt buộc.'\n321\t    if (!displayName.trim()) e.displayName = 'Tên hiển thị là {}\n337\t    if (subjects.length === 0) e.subjects = 'Vui lòng thêm ít nhất một môn học.'\n338\t    if (!education) e.education = 'Vui lòng chọn trình độ học vấn.'\n339\t    if (!experienceYears && ') }))\n358\t    else setErrors(prev => ({ ...prev, certificate: '' }))\n359\t    setCertificateFiles(prev => [...prev, ...valid])\n360\t  }, [])\n361\t\n362\t  const removeCertificate = 'Ảnh CCCD / CMND là bắt buộc.'\n371\t    setErrors(e)\n372\t    return Object.keys(e).length === 0\n373\t  }\n374\t\n375\t  const handleContinue = () => {\n376\t    let valid = false\n377\t    if (step === 1) valid = validateStep1()\n378\t    else if (step === 2) valid = validateStep2()\n379\t    else if (step === 3) valid = validateStep3()\n380\t    if (valid) {\n381\t      setStep(s => s + 1)\n382\t      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })\n383\t    }\n384\t  }\n385\t\n386\t  const handleBack = () => {\n387\t    setErrors({})\n388\t    setStep(s => s - 1)\n389\t    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })\n390\t  }\n391\t\n392\t  // ── Submit ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n393\t  const handleSubmit = async () => {\n394\t    if (!tosAccepted) {\n395\t      setErrors({ tos: 'Bạn phải đồng ý với Điều Khoản Dịch Vụ.' })\n396\t      return\n397\t    }\n398\t    setSubmitting(true)\n399\t    setSubmitMessage({ text: '', type: '' })\n400\t\n401\t    const formData = new FormData()\n402\t    // Step 1\n403\t    formData.append('first_name', firstName.trim())\n404\t    formData.append('last_name', lastName.trim())\n405\t    formData.append('display_name', displayName.trim())\n406\t    formData.append('birthday', `${birthYear}-${String(MONTHS.indexOf(birthMonth) + 1).padStart(2,  try {\n426\t      let activeToken = token\n427\t\n428\t      // ── Luồng đăng ký mới: tạo tài khoản trước, rồi mới tạo profile ────────\n429\t      if (isNewRegistration) {\n430\t        const regRes }),\n439\t        })\n440\t        const regData = await regRes.json()\n441\t        if (!regRes.ok) throw new Error(regData.message || 'Registration failed.')\n442\t        activeToken =
